## Change log rev 2 → rev 3

Every finding in `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-11-rbac-plan-234-codex-gate-r2.md` that names this plan, with **what rev 3 actually did**. A rejected finding says so with its evidence.

### BLOCKERs

| ID | Finding | What rev 3 did | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| **B1** | The command-level rerun is not scoped to R0's tenant; the row-count query uses the default connection | **Scoped both.** `R0` writes `tenant_id` into the ledger; Task 4-5 step 2 reads it with `jq`, refuses if it is absent, and passes **`--tenants="$TENANT"`** to all three `permissions:sync-fleet` invocations — wave 0b's selector contract, and §4.3.3 step 1a's one-tenant rule. The per-table counts move **inside `tenants:run --tenants="$TENANT"`**, with the reason spelled out: under db-per-tenant the default connection is the **central** database, which carries none of `roles`, `role_has_permissions` or `user_company_memberships` as the tenant's, so rev 2's three assertions were being taken against a database the campaign never touched. Added the committed-helper fallback if `tenants:run` cannot take an inline `--execute`, and made the dry-run's *"writes nothing"* a **query-log count** rather than an exit code | Task 4-5 Step 2 (rewritten); verification checklist |
| **B2** | `--reuse` cannot express the rule it is meant to enforce — there is no leg selector | **Added `--leg <id>`** to the parser, validated against the manifest's `legs:` block, and turned the refusal into a five-row outcome table: no `--leg` → `reuse_requires_leg`; two `--leg` → `reuse_is_single_leg`; `--leg` plus `--lanes` → `reuse_is_single_leg`; an unknown id → `unknown_leg`; one valid id → that leg alone. Rev 2's parser had `--reuse`, `--lanes` and `--row` and **no notion of a leg at all**, while the default run has nine and `--row` selects a different axis | Global Constraints (reuse table + the five outcomes); Task 4-3 Step 10 item 3; verification checklist |
| **B3** | The manifest schema cannot represent the lane table — one scalar `lane`, one scalar `command` | **`homes:` is a LIST.** Every row carries one or more `{lane, command, evidence}` entries and the runner executes every element. Rev 2's ad-hoc `also:` field — used on exactly one row, and **without a command**, so the second home ran nothing — is gone. Rule 5 of the manifest's guards makes an empty `homes:` fail, which is what *"every row has an executable home"* was supposed to guarantee. Nine rows in the worked YAML now carry two or three homes each | Task 4-2 Step 4 (schema + the six rules) |
| **B4** | The runner would execute shared mutating browser legs repeatedly | **Introduced a `legs:` block as a first-class entity.** Nine legs with ids, commands, `destructive:` flags and `covers:` lists; rows reference one by `leg:`. **The runner's six-step execution model** is stated explicitly: collect homes, partition, **dedup leg references by leg id and execute each leg ONCE**, dedup identical `(lane, command)` pairs, accumulate, never run a destructive leg twice. A leg's result is attributed to **every** referencing row. Counted the consequence of not doing this: **thirty-one leg references across twelve rows**, so R2 would have run four times, R4 six, R8 four — on a no-teardown single-use tenant. Eight of the nine legs are `destructive: true` | Task 4-2 Step 4 (`legs:` block + the execution model); Task 4-3 Step 10 item 2; verification checklist |
| **B5** | The staging replay does not execute the non-browser lanes against staging | **Made each lane's staging path explicit, per lane.** A seven-row table: **pg / redis / shell / queue-dependent rows execute over `ssh` against the deployed tree** (and `docker exec` for the entrypoint script, which only exists in the container); **vitest and posvitest declare themselves `local-only`** with the reason staging cannot show anything a local run does not; **playwright is `not replayed`** because its legs are destructive and the campaign is single-run. Added **`--ssh`, `--remote-root`, `--api-container`**, with `--staging-replay` **exiting 2** without `--ssh` rather than silently re-running the local lanes. Every ledger entry gains `execution_target`, the evidence table renders that field rather than a typed *"yes"*, and **a `local` target in a staging-replay run is a RED** | Task 4-5 Step 4 (the lane table, the invocation, `execution_target`); verification checklist |

### MAJORs

| ID | Finding | What rev 3 did | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | The EC-32d command resolves from the wrong directory | **`lanes.shell.cwd` becomes `apps/api`**, matching where the script is staged (`apps/api/tests/EdgeCases/entrypoint-boot-status.sh`), so `command: tests/EdgeCases/entrypoint-boot-status.sh` resolves. Manifest rule 2 now says commands resolve **relative to `lanes.<lane>.cwd`** and the guard checks it there, which is what would have caught this | Task 4-2 Step 4 (lanes block + rule 2) |
| **2** | The manifest is still a prose placeholder — "one entry per enumerated id" | **Supplied the complete 57-row listing** as a table of `id → homes → staging_replay`, in register order, with the leg references named. Stated the totals — 57 rows, 9 legs, 31 leg references across 12 rows — and kept Phase 0's reconciliation as the authority, with `RbacCampaignManifestCoverageTest` asserting equality so a difference is a finding rather than a drift | Task 4-2 Step 4 (the complete table); verification checklist |
| **3** | Task 4-2's add list contains a wildcard placeholder | **Replaced `<each new tests/Feature/EdgeCases/Rbac/*.php file, named>`** with the `git status --porcelain … \| awk` command that produces the list, the instruction to paste its output into both the add list and the commit body, and the note that the expected count is the **coverage delta's, not 57** — and that an empty delta is a legitimate outcome the body states rather than leaves unexplained | Task 4-2 Step 6 |
| **4** | The shared-library extraction premise is inaccurate | **Read `campaign-onboarding.sh:1-63` (rev 6 corrects the `:1-70` overrun on a 63-line file) and stated what it actually contains**, line-cited: arg parsing with a two-arg guard and unknown-argument `exit 2` (`:9-29`), two `curl --fail` preflights (`:31-39`), run-id and four exports (`:41-45`), the fiscal fail-fast (`:50-55`), the capture-and-exit contract (`:58-66`). **It has no `--reuse`, no `--lanes`, no `--row`, no `--leg` and no ledger accumulator** — the ledger is written by the Playwright project and the script only prints its path. Rev 3 therefore separates **EXTRACTED** (parity-tested) from **NEW** (additive, RBAC-only), and widens `CampaignLibParityTest` from two cases to **four** — help, unknown-argument `exit 2`, missing-value `exit 2`, preflight-failure `exit 1` — because rev 2's two left the arg-parse half of the extraction unproven | File Structure (*Modified* table); Q-w4-3; Task 4-3 Step 10 item 1 |
| **5** | R4's RETIRED arm requires a direct tenant-database query but defines no access path | **Moved the assertion to the lane that can make it.** The row-absence check leaves the browser leg and becomes `apps/api/tests/Feature/EdgeCases/Rbac/RetiredKeyAbsenceTest.php`, a `pg` home on EC-5 — which under `--staging-replay` runs on the **deployed** database over `ssh` (B5's lane table) rather than on a local one. R4 keeps the two assertions a browser genuinely can make: absence from `GET /api/v1/permissions`' payload, and no struck-through matrix entry. Stated the principle: **a leg that claims a database fact it cannot read is the same defect as one claiming a queue fact it cannot observe** (EC-16a, M4-3), and is fixed the same way | Task 4-3 Step 4 (the branch table + the new note); Task 4-3 Step 12 add list |

### Q-w disposition

No Q-w point moved. **Q-w4-3 gains MAJOR 4's accurate extraction inventory**, **Q-w4-4's no-teardown argument is now what B4's `destructive:` flags encode**, and **Q-w4-5's third answer (*observable, but not by this harness*) is what MAJOR 5 applies to R4's database assertion** — none is a re-decision. The gate's own reading, that no wave-4 finding escalates to a fifth owner question, is adopted.

### Rejected

**One, with evidence.**

- **The citation-audit item asking the base header to carry the current `dev` SHA is REJECTED as written**, on the same grounds as waves 2 and 3. The header records the SHA the `dev:` citations were **measured at** — *"Every `dev:` citation was verified against that SHA and **will have moved** — Phase 0 re-pins"* — and it already says the citations will have moved, which is the opposite of a currency claim. The gate's own audit confirms the cited code has not changed. Re-stamping without re-measuring would make it an unverified assertion. Phase 0 re-pins; the handback records what moved.

Everything else in the r2 register reproduces and was applied. Verifications worth recording:

- **B1** — `apps/api/config/database.php`'s default connection under db-per-tenant is the **central** one until `DatabaseTenancyBootstrapper` swaps it per request; an artisan `tinker` outside `tenants:run` never gets that swap, so rev 2's five `DB::table(...)->count()` calls would have hit `synerivia_central`.
- **B2** — rev 2's own Task 4-3 Step 10 item 1 lists the parser's flags: `--web`, `--api`, `--country`, `--reuse`, `--lanes`, `--row`. No `--leg`.
- **B4** — counting `leg:` references across the lane-assignment table gives R2 ×4, R3 ×3, R4 ×6, R5 ×2, R6 ×2, R7 ×1, R8 ×4, plus R0 and R1 — *rev 3 wrote "thirty-one references" here; the arithmetic is **22** across **22** rows, corrected in rev 4 (gate r3, Wave 4 **MAJOR 3**) and now asserted from the parsed manifest rather than counted by hand.* Nine legs.
- **B5** — `dev:scripts/campaign-onboarding.sh:5-6` exports `CAMPAIGN_API_URL` / `CAMPAIGN_WEB_URL` and nothing else consumes `--api`/`--web`; the Playwright project reads them. No non-browser command in rev 2's manifest reads either.
- **MAJOR 4** — `dev:scripts/campaign-onboarding.sh` `grep -c -- '--reuse\|--lanes\|--row\|ledger_'` returns **0**; the only `ledger` mention is the `echo` at `:63`.
- **minor 1** — `git show dev:apps/api/app/Enums/Vertical.php \| grep -n 'case '` lists `Pharmacy = 'pharmacy'` at `:20` and `Parapharmacy = 'parapharmacy'` at `:30`; `docs/qa/ONBOARDING-CAMPAIGN.md:22` names Parapharmacy and gives the batch-tracking reason.

| **B4-2** | The required same-tenant end-to-end rerun has no executable semantics | **ACCEPTED — option (b) implemented.** The campaign is declared **single-run-per-fresh-tenant**: `R0` re-registers every run, and `--reuse` is reserved for single-leg debugging, mirroring `docs/qa/ONBOARDING-CAMPAIGN.md`'s *Reuse mode* — and goes one step further by **refusing** a multi-leg reuse run (`exit 2`, `reason=reuse_is_single_leg`) rather than documenting a caveat. The master plan's exit is discharged at the **command** level in Task 4-5 step 2 (two extra `permissions:sync-fleet` runs against R0's tenant plus per-table row counts before and after), which is what its three assertions actually measure. Four guards: `RbacCampaignManifestCoverageTest` (ids equal the register), every `command` resolves (including `--filter` dry-runs), `lane: manual` rows carry a `result` or a `doc`, and `staging_replay: required` generates Task 4-5 step 4's table. ### Q-w disposition (gate's own reading, applied)

`Q-w4-1` was named as *"must be corrected or re-pinned"* and its ordinal premise is corrected in the Global Constraints. `Q-w4-2` … `Q-w4-5` stand as plan-author decisions, with **Q-w4-3**, **Q-w4-4** and **Q-w4-5** extended by M4-2, B4-2 and M4-1 respectively. **No wave-4 finding escalates to a fifth owner question**; the accepted four (OQ-1 … OQ-4) are settled or owner-owed by the time this wave runs.

### Rejected

**None.** Every wave-4 finding reproduces. The gate's own **rejected false positive** is retained as rev 1 had it:

- **The 57/58 register explanation is valid** — there are 57 active table ids and the prose reaches 58 by retaining withdrawn EC-22. Q-w4-2 stands, and Phase 0 still reconciles the list rather than assuming either number.

Verifications worth recording:

- **M4-1** — `apps/pos/package.json`'s `scripts` block contains `"test": "vitest run"` and no Playwright entry; no `@playwright/test` dependency is declared. A leg driving *"the dev IziPOS build"* had nothing to drive it with.
- **m4-2** — `sed -n '45,56p' docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md` puts the data-meaning sentence at 49–50 and `### The architecture ratchet (the mechanical half)` at 52.
- **B4-2** — `docs/qa/ONBOARDING-CAMPAIGN.md`'s *Reuse mode* section states reuse *"is for iterating on a single leg, not for a green run"* and that a tenant past L5 cannot re-run L1–L4 meaningfully. Rev 1's leg was the case that document warns against.
